Foreword
The Institute of Chartered Accountants of Bangladesh (ICAB) is the sole apex
body responsibleforregulatingthe professionof Accountants and matters
connected therewith in Bangladesh. The education and qualification imparted by
the ICAB in Accountancy is recognized as the highest professional education and
qualification in the country. The members of the ICAB are known as Chartered
Accountants and they are allowed to use the designatory letters ACA or FCA as
granted by the Institute.
ICAB was established under the Bangladesh Chartered Accountants Order 1973
(P.O.No.2 of 1973). Institute initially started functioning with only 78 members
which has now grown to a membership size of 2034.ICAB has regional offices in
Dhaka, Chattogram, Rajshahi, Rangpur, Khulna, Barishal, Sylhet and Mymensingh.
There are three overseas chapters of Dhaka Regional Committee i.e. one in
London and others in Toronto and Sydney.
ICAB is the primary regulator of Accounting & Auditing profession in the country
with mottos of ensuring transparency, accountability,integrityand good
governance in the financial sector of the economy of Bangladesh.
The responsibilities of the Institute are to conduct the professional examinations,
award membership of the Institute to qualified Chartered Accountants, issuance
of Practicing Certificates(CoP) to the members, maintain the status and standard
of professional qualifications and regulate the members of the Institute within the
purview of the Bangladesh Chartered Accountants Order 1973 and the Institute of
Chartered Accountants of Bangladesh Bye-laws,1973 (as amended up to 31
August 2023 and insertions thereafter). In addition, the Institute has to make
liaison with variousGovernment agencies,localand international Accounting
bodies and business houses. Ithas alsoto organize trainingcourses for its
members, students and other institutions to update and enrich their knowledge
on the profession of accountancy, auditing and information technology and legal
and statutory obligations and so on.
ICAB is a permanent member of several international and regional accounting
bodies includingthe IFAC (InternationalFederation of Accountants), IASB
(International Accounting Standards Board), CAPA (Confederation of Asian and
Pacific Accountants)and SAFA (South Asian Federation of Accountants). It
regularly participates in international and regional seminars and workshops across
the world. It also hosts such seminars and workshops in the country.
More than one-fourth of the Institute’s active membership is currently in public
practice, working inaccountancy firmsof all sizesfrom sole practitioners to
affiliates of large international firms and partnerships. The rest of the members are
employed in industry,financeand commerce and playing a wide variety of
financial, management and senior policymaking roles-both at home and abroad.
The Institute is governed by a Council comprising 20 members elected from the
two regional constituencies in Bangladesh headed by an elected President who is
the Chief Executive of the Council. The term of the council is for 3 years & period
of the presidency is for 1 year. The Council’s main responsibilities are the overall
management of the affairs and the business of the Institute including formulation,
setting and review of strategic policies. It provides advice and support to the
Government and various other stockholders when requested.
The day-to-day running of the Institute is delegated to the Secretariat, headed by
the Chief Executive Officer (CEO). The Secretariat is located at CA Bhaban, 100
Kazi Nazrul Islam Avenue, Kawran Bazar, Dhaka-1215, Bangladesh. All
divisions/departments can be contacted on the phones: 880-9609612100,
880-2-9115340, 9117521.

Explanatory Notes
i. Members of the Institute are entitled to describe themselves as
Chartered Accountants.
ii. ACA denotes an Associate Member of the Institute.
iii. FCA denotes a Fellow Member of the Institute.
iv. The year in brackets, immediately after the name of a member,
denotes his/her year of admission into the Institute.
v. The date in bracket, after the designatory letters ACA/FCA, denotes
the
date he/she became an ACA/FCA
vi. The figure in bracket, at the end of the name of each member,
denoteshis/her enrolment number.
Key
* - indicate a member’s entitlement to train articled students
(Ph) - telephone
(O) - office telephone
(R) - residence telephone
(M) - mobile
(F) - fax
(E) - e-mail
(W) - website

Format for Change of Address
For any change of address in the nextList of Members & Firmsas on 1st July
2026, please mail this format so as to reach the Institute by June 15, 2026. If
this format is not returned by the due date. It will be presumed that you do not
want any change in the entries against you name as printed in this issue.
Format
Name………………………………………………………………...........................................................………………..
Enrl. No.………………………………………………………………...........................................................…………
Correct Address(Mobile No./E-mail/Photo/Designation/Registered Address etc.
………………………………………………………………...........................................................……............…………..
………………………………………………………………...........................................................……............…………..
Date………………………………………………………………...........................................................……............…
(Signature of Member)
Send it(Hard Copy)
To
The CEO
The Institute of Chartered Accountants of Bangladesh
CA Bhaban
100 Kazi Nazrul Avenue, Kawran Bazar, Dhaka 1215
Or

as well as ICAB website.
